they answered: that which the lord has spoken to your servants, so will we do; we will pass over armed before the lord, and with us the possession of our inheritance — the commission named, the second doubled condition, moses' word the lord's; moses gave to gad, reuben and half the tribe of manasseh the kingdom of sihon and the kingdom of og, the land with its cities; gad built dibon, ataroth, aroer, atroth shophan, jazer, jogbehah, beth nimrah and beth haran — fortified cities and sheepfolds; the sons of machir son of manasseh went to gilead, took it and dispossessed the amorite; moses gave gilead to machir and he dwelt in it; jair son of manasseh took their villages and called them havvoth jair; nobah took kenath and its daughters and called it nobah after his own name — joseph's knees, the father of gilead, jair's two lineages, the daughter-towns.
